    WHEN (`h0`.`employee_id` = `h`.`employee_id`) AND (EXTRACT(day FROM `h0`.`pay_period_start`) = 16) THEN `h0`.`sss_deduction`
END), 0.0) AS `deductions`, (
    SELECT `e7`.`monthTotalWage`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h15`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e7` ON `h15`.`employee_id` = `e7`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h16` ON `e7`.`employeeId` = `h16`.`employee_id`

    LIMIT 1) AS `netPay`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`employeeId`
INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h0` ON `e`.`employeeId` = `h0`.`employee_id`
WHERE (`e`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e`.`year` = @__year_1)
GROUP BY `h`.`employee_id`

    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h8` ON `e3`.`employeeId` = `h8`.`employee_id`
    WHERE (((`e3`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e3`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h7`.`employee_id`)) AND (`h7`.`employee_id` = `h`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `department`, (
    SELECT `h9`.`role`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h9`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e4` ON `h9`.`employee_id` = `e4`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h10` ON `e4`.`employeeId` = `h10`.`employee_id`
    WHERE (((`e4`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e4`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h9`.`employee_id`)) AND (`h9`.`employee_id` = `h`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `role`, (

    LIMIT 1) AS `basicSalary`, (

    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e6` ON `h13`.`employee_id` = `e6`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h14` ON `e6`.`employeeId` = `h14`.`employee_id`

    LIMIT 1) AS `overtimePay`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN (`h0`.`employee_id` = `h`.`employee_id`) AND (EXTRACT(day FROM `h0`.`pay_period_start`) = 16) THEN `h0`.`sss_deduction`
END), 0.0) AS `deductions`, (

    LIMIT 1) AS `netPay`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`employeeId`
INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h0` ON `e`.`employeeId` = `h0`.`employee_id`
WHERE (`e`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e`.`year` = @__year_1)
GROUP BY `h`.`employee_id`
